{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_01BnQskz4uuoYeyLyQhuBGeJ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013uyZHApS2XRVXWSRpnGvYw","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/design_doc.md","content":"# Priority-Based Shard Allocation Strategy Design\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document describes the design of a new `PriorityShardsAllocator` component for Elasticsearch that extends the existing shard allocation framework to support **priority-aware allocation**. The allocator enables critical indices to be preferentially placed on faster (higher-capacity or better-performing) nodes within the cluster.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Current Architecture Overview\n\n### 1.1 Existing Allocation Framework\n\nElasticsearch's shard allocation is based on a **pluggable architecture** with three core components:\n\n#### **ShardsAllocator Interface**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/ShardsAllocator.java`\n- **Responsibilities**:\n  - `allocate(RoutingAllocation)` - Main allocation method\n  - `allocate(RoutingAllocation, ActionListener<Void>)` - Async variant\n  - `decideShardAllocation(ShardRouting, RoutingAllocation)` - Explain API support\n  - `execute(RoutingAllocation, AllocationCommands, ...)` - Execute explicit commands\n\n#### **BalancedShardsAllocator**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/BalancedShardsAllocator.java`\n- **Current Implementation**: Balances shards using weighted function approach\n- **Balance Factors**:\n  - Shard count balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.shard)\n  - Index spread balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.index)\n  - Write load balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.write_load)\n  - Disk usage balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.disk_usage)\n- **Design Pattern**: Uses WeightFunction to compute node scores; allocates to node with highest score\n\n#### **AllocationDeciders Composition**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AllocationDeciders.java`\n- **Purpose**: Combines multiple AllocationDecider instances using **fail-fast AND logic**\n  - Returns YES (proceed), THROTTLE (rate-limit), or NO (reject)\n  - First NO result blocks allocation\n- **Methods**:\n  - `canAllocate(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)`\n  - `canRebalance(ShardRouting, RoutingAllocation)`\n  - `canRemain(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)`\n  - `shouldAutoExpandToNode(IndexMetadata, DiscoveryNode, RoutingAllocation)`\n\n#### **AllocationDecider Base Class**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AllocationDecider.java`\n- **Design**: Abstract base class with decision methods returning Decision objects\n- **Key Methods**:\n  - `canAllocate(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)` - allocation decision\n  - `canRemain(IndexMetadata, ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)` - residency decision\n  - `canRebalance(ShardRouting, RoutingAllocation)` - rebalancing permission\n  - `canForceAllocatePrimary(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)` - force-allocate on lost node\n  - All methods default to `Decision.ALWAYS` if not overridden\n\n### 1.2 Existing Decider Examples\n\n#### **DiskThresholdDecider**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/DiskThresholdDecider.java`\n- **Purpose**: Prevents allocation to disk-full nodes\n- **Settings**:\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.low` - new allocations blocked above this\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.high` - shards must relocate above this\n  - `index.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.ignore` - per-index override\n\n#### **AwarenessAllocationDecider**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AwarenessAllocationDecider.java`\n- **Purpose**: Distributes replicas across rack/zone awareness attributes\n- **Settings**:\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.awareness.attributes` - which attributes to honor\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.awareness.force.{attribute}.values` - forced group sizes\n\n---\n\n## 2. Problem Statement & Goals\n\n### 2.1 Current Limitation\n\nToday, Elasticsearch allocates shards uniformly across all data nodes based on balance metrics (shard count, disk usage, write load). It does **not** consider:\n- Node performance characteristics (CPU, memory speed, disk type)\n- Index criticality or SLA requirements\n- Tiered node infrastructure (hot/warm/cold are tier-based, not performance-based)\n\n### 2.2 Desired Capability\n\n**Priority-aware allocation** allows operators to:\n1. Mark critical indices with a `priority` level (e.g., 1-100)\n2. Tag nodes with performance tiers (e.g., `node_tier: premium`, `node_tier: standard`)\n3. Have the allocator **prefer** placing high-priority shards on high-tier nodes\n4. Fall back to standard nodes if insufficient premium capacity exists\n\n### 2.3 Design Goals\n\n1. **Non-intrusive**: Integrate with existing allocator without major refactoring\n2. **Composable**: Work alongside existing deciders (DiskThreshold, Awareness, etc.)\n3. **Configurable**: Allow operators to tune priority behavior per-cluster\n4. **Observable**: Clear logging/explain API output for why shards were/weren't allocated\n5. **Safe**: Graceful degradation—critical shards still allocate even to lower-tier nodes\n\n---\n\n## 3. Proposed Design: PriorityShardsAllocator\n\n### 3.1 Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ AllocationService (cluster-wide routing orchestration)          │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                              │\n                              ▼\n                    ┌──────────────────────┐\n                    │ ShardsAllocator      │\n                    │ (pluggable)          │\n                    └──────────────────────┘\n                              │\n              ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐\n              ▼               ▼               ▼\n    BalancedShardsAllocator   │    PriorityShardsAllocator\n                         (new)│    (new - wrapper/extension)\n                              │\n              ┌───────────────┴───────────────┐\n              │   AllocationDeciders chain    │\n              └───────────────────────────────┘\n                    │  │  │  │  │  │  │\n    ┌───────────────┼──┼──┼──┼──┼──┼──┼───────────────┐\n    ▼               ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼               ▼\n Disk           Enable Awareness Same... Filter  PriorityAllocationDecider\n Threshold      Alloc  Decider   Shard           (new)\n Decider\n```\n\n### 3.2 Core Components\n\n#### **Component 1: Index Setting for Priority**\n\n**Name**: `index.routing.allocation.priority`\n**Type**: Integer\n**Default**: 50\n**Range**: 0-100\n**Scope**: Index\n**Dynamic**: Yes\n\n**Definition** (in `IndexMetadata.java`):\n```java\npublic static final String SETTING_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY = \"index.routing.allocation.priority\";\npublic static final Setting<Integer> INDEX_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_SETTING = Setting.intSetting(\n    \"index.routing.allocation.priority\",\n    50,  // default: medium priority\n    0,   // minimum\n    100, // maximum\n    Property.Dynamic,\n    Property.IndexScope\n);\n```\n\n**Usage Example**:\n```bash\nPUT /critical-app/_settings\n{\n  \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 100  # highest priority\n}\n\nPUT /cache-index/_settings\n{\n  \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 10   # lowest priority\n}\n```\n\n#### **Component 2: Node Attribute for Tier Classification**\n\n**Name**: `node.attr.allocation_tier` (operator-configured)\n**Example Values**: `premium`, `standard`, `basic`, `cold`\n\n**Configuration** (in elasticsearch.yml):\n```yaml\n# Premium nodes (fast SSD, 256GB RAM, 16 cores)\nnode.name: premium-node-1\nnode.attr.allocation_tier: premium\n\n# Standard nodes (regular SSD, 64GB RAM, 8 cores)\nnode.name: standard-node-1\nnode.attr.allocation_tier: standard\n```\n\n#### **Component 3: PriorityAllocationDecider**\n\n**Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/PriorityAllocationDecider.java`\n\n**Responsibilities**:\n1. Extract priority from IndexMetadata\n2. Classify node as `premium` or `standard` (configurable)\n3. Implement soft constraints via scoring adjustments\n4. Implement hard constraints (e.g., reject allocating high-priority to low-tier nodes if alternatives exist)\n\n**Key Methods**:\n```java\npublic class PriorityAllocationDecider extends AllocationDecider {\n    public static final Setting<Boolean> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_ENABLED =\n        Setting.boolSetting(\n            \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled\",\n            true,\n            Property.Dynamic,\n            Property.NodeScope\n        );\n\n    public static final Setting<List<String>> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PREMIUM_TIERS =\n        Setting.stringListSetting(\n            \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers\",\n            List.of(\"premium\"),\n            Property.Dynamic,\n            Property.NodeScope\n        );\n\n    @Override\n    public Decision canAllocate(ShardRouting shardRouting, RoutingNode node, RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        // If feature disabled, always allow\n        if (!enabled) return Decision.ALWAYS;\n\n        // Get shard's index priority\n        IndexMetadata indexMetadata = allocation.metadata().getIndexSafe(shardRouting.index());\n        int indexPriority = getIndexPriority(indexMetadata);\n\n        // Get node's tier classification\n        String nodeTier = getNodeTier(node);\n\n        // Decision logic:\n        // 1. If index priority >= threshold AND node is premium tier: YES\n        // 2. If index priority >= threshold AND node is standard: THROTTLE (soft constraint)\n        // 3. If index priority < threshold: YES (standard allocation)\n\n        return decideAllocation(indexPriority, nodeTier);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### **Component 4: PriorityShardsAllocator (Optional Extension)**\n\n**Purpose**: A wrapper allocator that prioritizes shards by priority level\n**Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/PriorityShardsAllocator.java`\n\n**Design**:\n- Wraps or extends BalancedShardsAllocator\n- Reorders shard assignment queue by priority (highest-priority shards assigned first)\n- Delegates actual balancing to BalancedShardsAllocator\n- Ensures high-priority shards get \"first pick\" of available slots\n\n```java\npublic class PriorityShardsAllocator implements ShardsAllocator {\n    private final BalancedShardsAllocator delegate;\n\n    @Override\n    public void allocate(RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        // Sort unassigned shards by priority (descending)\n        sortUnassignedShardsByPriority(allocation);\n\n        // Delegate to BalancedShardsAllocator for actual allocation\n        delegate.allocate(allocation);\n    }\n\n    private void sortUnassignedShardsByPriority(RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        // Extract index priorities and reorder routing nodes' shard queues\n        RoutingNodes routingNodes = allocation.routingNodes();\n        routingNodes.unassigned().sort((shard1, shard2) -> {\n            int priority1 = getIndexPriority(allocation, shard1);\n            int priority2 = getIndexPriority(allocation, shard2);\n            return Integer.compare(priority2, priority1); // descending\n        });\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Integration with Existing Components\n\n### 4.1 AllocationDeciders Composition\n\n**Where PriorityAllocationDecider Fits**:\n\nThe PriorityAllocationDecider is registered in the decider chain (in AllocationService or RoutingAllocation initialization):\n\n```\nAllocationDeciders chain:\n1. EnableAllocationDecider        (check index.routing.allocation.enable)\n2. RebalanceOnlyWhenActiveDecider (wait for primaries to become active)\n3. DiskThresholdDecider           (check free disk space)\n4. AwarenessAllocationDecider     (check rack/zone awareness)\n5. FilterAllocationDecider        (check include/exclude/require filters)\n6. PriorityAllocationDecider      (NEW - apply priority preferences)\n7. SameShardAllocationDecider     (prevent primary/replica on same node)\n... other deciders ...\n```\n\n**Decision Logic**:\n- Each decider in sequence is queried via `canAllocate(shard, node, allocation)`\n- Chain uses **fail-fast AND semantics**: first NO result blocks the allocation\n- PriorityAllocationDecider returns THROTTLE for soft constraints (prefer premium nodes)\n- PriorityAllocationDecider returns YES/NO based on hard constraints (must allocate somewhere)\n\n### 4.2 Interaction with DiskThresholdDecider\n\n**Scenario**: High-priority index needs to allocate, but only disk-full nodes available\n\n**Behavior**:\n1. PriorityAllocationDecider allows allocation (priority >= some threshold)\n2. DiskThresholdDecider may still block (disk above watermark)\n3. **Result**: Shard stays unassigned or uses force-allocate path\n4. **Policy**: Disk constraints take precedence over priority preferences (safety first)\n\n### 4.3 Interaction with AwarenessAllocationDecider\n\n**Scenario**: High-priority index with awareness requirement (e.g., replicas on different racks)\n\n**Behavior**:\n1. AwarenessAllocationDecider enforces rack/zone distribution first\n2. Within rack constraints, PriorityAllocationDecider prefers premium nodes\n3. **Example**:\n   - Rack A: 1 premium node (100MB free), 2 standard nodes (1GB free each)\n   - High-priority shard for Rack A chooses premium node\n   - (assumes disk space sufficient in all)\n\n---\n\n## 5. API Changes & Additions\n\n### 5.1 Index Settings\n\n**New Setting**: `index.routing.allocation.priority`\n- Type: Integer (0-100)\n- Default: 50\n- Scope: Index\n- Dynamic: Yes\n- API: `PUT /index/_settings { \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 75 }`\n\n### 5.2 Cluster Settings\n\n**New Settings**:\n\n| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |\n|---------|------|---------|---------|\n| `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled` | Boolean | true | Enable/disable feature |\n| `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers` | List[String] | [\"premium\"] | Node tier names considered premium |\n| `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold` | Integer | 70 | Priority value above which prefer premium nodes |\n\n### 5.3 Cluster Explain API Enhancement\n\n**Extended Output** (from existing `GET /_cluster/allocation/explain`):\n\n```json\n{\n  \"shard\": {\n    \"index\": \"critical-app\",\n    \"index_priority\": 100,\n    \"id\": 0,\n    \"primary\": true\n  },\n  \"assigned\": false,\n  \"allocation_delay\": \"0ms\",\n  \"remaining_delay\": \"...\",\n  \"decisions\": [\n    {\n      \"decider\": \"enable\",\n      \"decision\": \"YES\",\n      \"explanation\": \"all allocation decisions are enabled\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"decider\": \"priority_allocation\",\n      \"decision\": \"THROTTLE\",\n      \"explanation\": \"shard has high priority (100); prefer premium tier nodes, but none available; will allocate to standard node\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"decider\": \"disk_threshold\",\n      \"decision\": \"YES\",\n      \"explanation\": \"enough disk space available\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Implementation Roadmap\n\n### Phase 1: Foundation\n- Add `INDEX_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_SETTING` to IndexMetadata\n- Create `PriorityAllocationDecider` with basic logic\n- Register decider in AllocationService\n\n### Phase 2: Allocator Extension\n- Create `PriorityShardsAllocator` wrapper\n- Implement shard queue reordering\n- Add configuration settings for tiers\n\n### Phase 3: Integration & Testing\n- Update Explain API to show priority decisions\n- Add cluster/index-level settings\n- Comprehensive test suite (unit + integration)\n\n### Phase 4: Documentation & Observability\n- Operator documentation (how to enable, configure)\n- Troubleshooting guides\n- Metrics for priority allocation success rates\n\n---\n\n## 7. Behavior Examples\n\n### Example 1: Simple Priority Allocation\n\n**Cluster Setup**:\n- Node-1: `allocation_tier=premium`, 500GB disk\n- Node-2: `allocation_tier=standard`, 500GB disk\n\n**Index Setup**:\n- `critical-index`: priority=100\n- `cache-index`: priority=10\n\n**Allocation Behavior**:\n1. `critical-index` shards allocate first and prefer Node-1 (premium)\n2. `cache-index` shards allocate to remaining space, can use Node-2 (standard)\n3. If Node-1 fills up, `critical-index` shards still allocate to Node-2\n\n### Example 2: Awareness + Priority\n\n**Cluster Setup**:\n- Rack A: premium-node-1, standard-node-2\n- Rack B: premium-node-3, standard-node-4\n\n**Replication Constraint**: Replicas must be on different racks\n**High-Priority Shard**: priority=90\n\n**Allocation**:\n1. Primary → Rack A's premium-node-1\n2. Replica → Rack B's premium-node-3 (respects awareness + preference)\n\n### Example 3: Degradation Under Load\n\n**Cluster Setup**:\n- 2 premium nodes (90% full)\n- 3 standard nodes (10% full)\n\n**Incoming Shard**: priority=100\n\n**Behavior**:\n1. Try premium nodes first (via THROTTLE decision)\n2. Premium nodes full, allocation blocked\n3. Fall back to standard nodes (via force-allocate or next phase)\n4. Shard allocates to standard node (graceful degradation)\n\n---\n\n## 8. Backward Compatibility\n\n### 8.1 Default Behavior\n\n- Feature disabled by default (opt-in via settings)\n- All indices default to priority=50 (middle of range)\n- Nodes without `allocation_tier` attribute treated as standard tier\n- Existing allocation logic unchanged if feature is disabled\n\n### 8.2 Upgrade Path\n\n1. Deploy new Elasticsearch version\n2. Feature defaults to enabled but with neutral priority thresholds\n3. No change to existing cluster behavior\n4. Operators gradually enable priority constraints\n\n---\n\n## 9. Observability & Debugging\n\n### 9.1 Logging\n\nPriorityAllocationDecider logs at TRACE level:\n```\n[priority_allocation] shard [index/1], priority=90, trying node [node-1], tier=premium -> allowed\n[priority_allocation] shard [index/1], priority=90, trying node [node-2], tier=standard -> throttled (prefer premium)\n```\n\n### 9.2 Explain API\n\nExample query:\n```bash\nGET /_cluster/allocation/explain\n{\n  \"index\": \"critical-app\",\n  \"shard\": 0,\n  \"primary\": true\n}\n```\n\n### 9.3 Metrics\n\n(Future enhancement):\n- `elasticsearch.routing.allocation.priority.high_tier_allocations` - count of high-priority shards on premium nodes\n- `elasticsearch.routing.allocation.priority.degraded_allocations` - count of high-priority shards on standard nodes\n\n---\n\n## 10. Security Considerations\n\n1. **Index Priority as User Input**: Index priority is settable by any user with index permissions\n   - **Mitigation**: Operators can cap priority via role-based settings restrictions\n\n2. **Resource Starvation**: Low-priority indices might starve for premium node resources\n   - **Mitigation**: Set reasonable defaults; operators can adjust thresholds\n\n3. **Audit Trail**: Track priority-based allocation decisions for compliance\n   - **Mitigation**: Enhanced logging + explain API output\n\n---\n\n## 11. Performance Considerations\n\n1. **Shard Queue Sorting** (in PriorityShardsAllocator):\n   - O(n log n) operation once per allocation round\n   - n = number of unassigned shards (typically < 1000 in most clusters)\n   - Negligible impact\n\n2. **Decider Invocation** (in PriorityAllocationDecider):\n   - O(1) per shard-node pair (simple priority lookup + tier check)\n   - No additional cluster state lookups\n\n3. **Overall Impact**: Negligible; allocation is already O(n²) in worst case\n\n---\n\n## 12. Testing Strategy\n\n### Unit Tests\n- PriorityAllocationDecider decision logic\n- Priority extraction from IndexMetadata\n- Node tier classification\n\n### Integration Tests\n- Multi-node cluster allocation scenarios\n- Mixed priority indices\n- Awareness + priority interactions\n- Disk threshold + priority interactions\n\n### Chaos Tests\n- Node failures with high/low-priority indices\n- Dynamic priority/tier changes\n- Large unassigned shard queues\n\n---\n\n## 13. References\n\n### Existing Components\n- **ShardsAllocator**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/allocator/ShardsAllocator.java`\n- **BalancedShardsAllocator**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/allocator/BalancedShardsAllocator.java`\n- **AllocationDecider**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/AllocationDecider.java`\n- **AllocationDeciders**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/AllocationDeciders.java`\n- **DiskThresholdDecider**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/DiskThresholdDecider.java`\n- **AwarenessAllocationDecider**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/AwarenessAllocationDecider.java`\n\n### Configuration\n- **IndexMetadata**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/IndexMetadata.java` (settings definitions)\n- **ClusterSettings**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/common/settings/ClusterSettings.java` (cluster-wide settings)\n\n### Testing\n- **DesiredBalanceComputerTests**: `server/src/test/java/.../allocation/allocator/DesiredBalanceComputerTests.java`\n- **AwarenessAllocationDeciderTests**: (search tests directory)\n\n---\n\n## 14. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013uyZHApS2XRVXWSRpnGvYw","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /workspace/design_doc.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c7593c6d-2214-427f-b82a-76b12bde53dd","uuid":"1bbac4e4-c651-4637-af26-76cfa04b83b8","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/workspace/design_doc.md","content":"# Priority-Based Shard Allocation Strategy Design\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document describes the design of a new `PriorityShardsAllocator` component for Elasticsearch that extends the existing shard allocation framework to support **priority-aware allocation**. The allocator enables critical indices to be preferentially placed on faster (higher-capacity or better-performing) nodes within the cluster.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Current Architecture Overview\n\n### 1.1 Existing Allocation Framework\n\nElasticsearch's shard allocation is based on a **pluggable architecture** with three core components:\n\n#### **ShardsAllocator Interface**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/ShardsAllocator.java`\n- **Responsibilities**:\n  - `allocate(RoutingAllocation)` - Main allocation method\n  - `allocate(RoutingAllocation, ActionListener<Void>)` - Async variant\n  - `decideShardAllocation(ShardRouting, RoutingAllocation)` - Explain API support\n  - `execute(RoutingAllocation, AllocationCommands, ...)` - Execute explicit commands\n\n#### **BalancedShardsAllocator**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/BalancedShardsAllocator.java`\n- **Current Implementation**: Balances shards using weighted function approach\n- **Balance Factors**:\n  - Shard count balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.shard)\n  - Index spread balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.index)\n  - Write load balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.write_load)\n  - Disk usage balance (cluster.routing.allocation.balance.disk_usage)\n- **Design Pattern**: Uses WeightFunction to compute node scores; allocates to node with highest score\n\n#### **AllocationDeciders Composition**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AllocationDeciders.java`\n- **Purpose**: Combines multiple AllocationDecider instances using **fail-fast AND logic**\n  - Returns YES (proceed), THROTTLE (rate-limit), or NO (reject)\n  - First NO result blocks allocation\n- **Methods**:\n  - `canAllocate(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)`\n  - `canRebalance(ShardRouting, RoutingAllocation)`\n  - `canRemain(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)`\n  - `shouldAutoExpandToNode(IndexMetadata, DiscoveryNode, RoutingAllocation)`\n\n#### **AllocationDecider Base Class**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AllocationDecider.java`\n- **Design**: Abstract base class with decision methods returning Decision objects\n- **Key Methods**:\n  - `canAllocate(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)` - allocation decision\n  - `canRemain(IndexMetadata, ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)` - residency decision\n  - `canRebalance(ShardRouting, RoutingAllocation)` - rebalancing permission\n  - `canForceAllocatePrimary(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)` - force-allocate on lost node\n  - All methods default to `Decision.ALWAYS` if not overridden\n\n### 1.2 Existing Decider Examples\n\n#### **DiskThresholdDecider**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/DiskThresholdDecider.java`\n- **Purpose**: Prevents allocation to disk-full nodes\n- **Settings**:\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.low` - new allocations blocked above this\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.high` - shards must relocate above this\n  - `index.routing.allocation.disk.watermark.ignore` - per-index override\n\n#### **AwarenessAllocationDecider**\n- **Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AwarenessAllocationDecider.java`\n- **Purpose**: Distributes replicas across rack/zone awareness attributes\n- **Settings**:\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.awareness.attributes` - which attributes to honor\n  - `cluster.routing.allocation.awareness.force.{attribute}.values` - forced group sizes\n\n---\n\n## 2. Problem Statement & Goals\n\n### 2.1 Current Limitation\n\nToday, Elasticsearch allocates shards uniformly across all data nodes based on balance metrics (shard count, disk usage, write load). It does **not** consider:\n- Node performance characteristics (CPU, memory speed, disk type)\n- Index criticality or SLA requirements\n- Tiered node infrastructure (hot/warm/cold are tier-based, not performance-based)\n\n### 2.2 Desired Capability\n\n**Priority-aware allocation** allows operators to:\n1. Mark critical indices with a `priority` level (e.g., 1-100)\n2. Tag nodes with performance tiers (e.g., `node_tier: premium`, `node_tier: standard`)\n3. Have the allocator **prefer** placing high-priority shards on high-tier nodes\n4. Fall back to standard nodes if insufficient premium capacity exists\n\n### 2.3 Design Goals\n\n1. **Non-intrusive**: Integrate with existing allocator without major refactoring\n2. **Composable**: Work alongside existing deciders (DiskThreshold, Awareness, etc.)\n3. **Configurable**: Allow operators to tune priority behavior per-cluster\n4. **Observable**: Clear logging/explain API output for why shards were/weren't allocated\n5. **Safe**: Graceful degradation—critical shards still allocate even to lower-tier nodes\n\n---\n\n## 3. Proposed Design: PriorityShardsAllocator\n\n### 3.1 Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ AllocationService (cluster-wide routing orchestration)          │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                              │\n                              ▼\n                    ┌──────────────────────┐\n                    │ ShardsAllocator      │\n                    │ (pluggable)          │\n                    └──────────────────────┘\n                              │\n              ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐\n              ▼               ▼               ▼\n    BalancedShardsAllocator   │    PriorityShardsAllocator\n                         (new)│    (new - wrapper/extension)\n                              │\n              ┌───────────────┴───────────────┐\n              │   AllocationDeciders chain    │\n              └───────────────────────────────┘\n                    │  │  │  │  │  │  │\n    ┌───────────────┼──┼──┼──┼──┼──┼──┼───────────────┐\n    ▼               ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼  ▼               ▼\n Disk           Enable Awareness Same... Filter  PriorityAllocationDecider\n Threshold      Alloc  Decider   Shard           (new)\n Decider\n```\n\n### 3.2 Core Components\n\n#### **Component 1: Index Setting for Priority**\n\n**Name**: `index.routing.allocation.priority`\n**Type**: Integer\n**Default**: 50\n**Range**: 0-100\n**Scope**: Index\n**Dynamic**: Yes\n\n**Definition** (in `IndexMetadata.java`):\n```java\npublic static final String SETTING_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY = \"index.routing.allocation.priority\";\npublic static final Setting<Integer> INDEX_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_SETTING = Setting.intSetting(\n    \"index.routing.allocation.priority\",\n    50,  // default: medium priority\n    0,   // minimum\n    100, // maximum\n    Property.Dynamic,\n    Property.IndexScope\n);\n```\n\n**Usage Example**:\n```bash\nPUT /critical-app/_settings\n{\n  \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 100  # highest priority\n}\n\nPUT /cache-index/_settings\n{\n  \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 10   # lowest priority\n}\n```\n\n#### **Component 2: Node Attribute for Tier Classification**\n\n**Name**: `node.attr.allocation_tier` (operator-configured)\n**Example Values**: `premium`, `standard`, `basic`, `cold`\n\n**Configuration** (in elasticsearch.yml):\n```yaml\n# Premium nodes (fast SSD, 256GB RAM, 16 cores)\nnode.name: premium-node-1\nnode.attr.allocation_tier: premium\n\n# Standard nodes (regular SSD, 64GB RAM, 8 cores)\nnode.name: standard-node-1\nnode.attr.allocation_tier: standard\n```\n\n#### **Component 3: PriorityAllocationDecider**\n\n**Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/PriorityAllocationDecider.java`\n\n**Responsibilities**:\n1. Extract priority from IndexMetadata\n2. Classify node as `premium` or `standard` (configurable)\n3. Implement soft constraints via scoring adjustments\n4. Implement hard constraints (e.g., reject allocating high-priority to low-tier nodes if alternatives exist)\n\n**Key Methods**:\n```java\npublic class PriorityAllocationDecider extends AllocationDecider {\n    public static final Setting<Boolean> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_ENABLED =\n        Setting.boolSetting(\n            \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled\",\n            true,\n            Property.Dynamic,\n            Property.NodeScope\n        );\n\n    public static final Setting<List<String>> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PREMIUM_TIERS =\n        Setting.stringListSetting(\n            \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers\",\n            List.of(\"premium\"),\n            Property.Dynamic,\n            Property.NodeScope\n        );\n\n    @Override\n    public Decision canAllocate(ShardRouting shardRouting, RoutingNode node, RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        // If feature disabled, always allow\n        if (!enabled) return Decision.ALWAYS;\n\n        // Get shard's index priority\n        IndexMetadata indexMetadata = allocation.metadata().getIndexSafe(shardRouting.index());\n        int indexPriority = getIndexPriority(indexMetadata);\n\n        // Get node's tier classification\n        String nodeTier = getNodeTier(node);\n\n        // Decision logic:\n        // 1. If index priority >= threshold AND node is premium tier: YES\n        // 2. If index priority >= threshold AND node is standard: THROTTLE (soft constraint)\n        // 3. If index priority < threshold: YES (standard allocation)\n\n        return decideAllocation(indexPriority, nodeTier);\n    }\n}\n```\n\n#### **Component 4: PriorityShardsAllocator (Optional Extension)**\n\n**Purpose**: A wrapper allocator that prioritizes shards by priority level\n**Location**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/PriorityShardsAllocator.java`\n\n**Design**:\n- Wraps or extends BalancedShardsAllocator\n- Reorders shard assignment queue by priority (highest-priority shards assigned first)\n- Delegates actual balancing to BalancedShardsAllocator\n- Ensures high-priority shards get \"first pick\" of available slots\n\n```java\npublic class PriorityShardsAllocator implements ShardsAllocator {\n    private final BalancedShardsAllocator delegate;\n\n    @Override\n    public void allocate(RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        // Sort unassigned shards by priority (descending)\n        sortUnassignedShardsByPriority(allocation);\n\n        // Delegate to BalancedShardsAllocator for actual allocation\n        delegate.allocate(allocation);\n    }\n\n    private void sortUnassignedShardsByPriority(RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        // Extract index priorities and reorder routing nodes' shard queues\n        RoutingNodes routingNodes = allocation.routingNodes();\n        routingNodes.unassigned().sort((shard1, shard2) -> {\n            int priority1 = getIndexPriority(allocation, shard1);\n            int priority2 = getIndexPriority(allocation, shard2);\n            return Integer.compare(priority2, priority1); // descending\n        });\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Integration with Existing Components\n\n### 4.1 AllocationDeciders Composition\n\n**Where PriorityAllocationDecider Fits**:\n\nThe PriorityAllocationDecider is registered in the decider chain (in AllocationService or RoutingAllocation initialization):\n\n```\nAllocationDeciders chain:\n1. EnableAllocationDecider        (check index.routing.allocation.enable)\n2. RebalanceOnlyWhenActiveDecider (wait for primaries to become active)\n3. DiskThresholdDecider           (check free disk space)\n4. AwarenessAllocationDecider     (check rack/zone awareness)\n5. FilterAllocationDecider        (check include/exclude/require filters)\n6. PriorityAllocationDecider      (NEW - apply priority preferences)\n7. SameShardAllocationDecider     (prevent primary/replica on same node)\n... other deciders ...\n```\n\n**Decision Logic**:\n- Each decider in sequence is queried via `canAllocate(shard, node, allocation)`\n- Chain uses **fail-fast AND semantics**: first NO result blocks the allocation\n- PriorityAllocationDecider returns THROTTLE for soft constraints (prefer premium nodes)\n- PriorityAllocationDecider returns YES/NO based on hard constraints (must allocate somewhere)\n\n### 4.2 Interaction with DiskThresholdDecider\n\n**Scenario**: High-priority index needs to allocate, but only disk-full nodes available\n\n**Behavior**:\n1. PriorityAllocationDecider allows allocation (priority >= some threshold)\n2. DiskThresholdDecider may still block (disk above watermark)\n3. **Result**: Shard stays unassigned or uses force-allocate path\n4. **Policy**: Disk constraints take precedence over priority preferences (safety first)\n\n### 4.3 Interaction with AwarenessAllocationDecider\n\n**Scenario**: High-priority index with awareness requirement (e.g., replicas on different racks)\n\n**Behavior**:\n1. AwarenessAllocationDecider enforces rack/zone distribution first\n2. Within rack constraints, PriorityAllocationDecider prefers premium nodes\n3. **Example**:\n   - Rack A: 1 premium node (100MB free), 2 standard nodes (1GB free each)\n   - High-priority shard for Rack A chooses premium node\n   - (assumes disk space sufficient in all)\n\n---\n\n## 5. API Changes & Additions\n\n### 5.1 Index Settings\n\n**New Setting**: `index.routing.allocation.priority`\n- Type: Integer (0-100)\n- Default: 50\n- Scope: Index\n- Dynamic: Yes\n- API: `PUT /index/_settings { \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 75 }`\n\n### 5.2 Cluster Settings\n\n**New Settings**:\n\n| Setting | Type | Default | Purpose |\n|---------|------|---------|---------|\n| `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled` | Boolean | true | Enable/disable feature |\n| `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers` | List[String] | [\"premium\"] | Node tier names considered premium |\n| `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold` | Integer | 70 | Priority value above which prefer premium nodes |\n\n### 5.3 Cluster Explain API Enhancement\n\n**Extended Output** (from existing `GET /_cluster/allocation/explain`):\n\n```json\n{\n  \"shard\": {\n    \"index\": \"critical-app\",\n    \"index_priority\": 100,\n    \"id\": 0,\n    \"primary\": true\n  },\n  \"assigned\": false,\n  \"allocation_delay\": \"0ms\",\n  \"remaining_delay\": \"...\",\n  \"decisions\": [\n    {\n      \"decider\": \"enable\",\n      \"decision\": \"YES\",\n      \"explanation\": \"all allocation decisions are enabled\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"decider\": \"priority_allocation\",\n      \"decision\": \"THROTTLE\",\n      \"explanation\": \"shard has high priority (100); prefer premium tier nodes, but none available; will allocate to standard node\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"decider\": \"disk_threshold\",\n      \"decision\": \"YES\",\n      \"explanation\": \"enough disk space available\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Implementation Roadmap\n\n### Phase 1: Foundation\n- Add `INDEX_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_SETTING` to IndexMetadata\n- Create `PriorityAllocationDecider` with basic logic\n- Register decider in AllocationService\n\n### Phase 2: Allocator Extension\n- Create `PriorityShardsAllocator` wrapper\n- Implement shard queue reordering\n- Add configuration settings for tiers\n\n### Phase 3: Integration & Testing\n- Update Explain API to show priority decisions\n- Add cluster/index-level settings\n- Comprehensive test suite (unit + integration)\n\n### Phase 4: Documentation & Observability\n- Operator documentation (how to enable, configure)\n- Troubleshooting guides\n- Metrics for priority allocation success rates\n\n---\n\n## 7. Behavior Examples\n\n### Example 1: Simple Priority Allocation\n\n**Cluster Setup**:\n- Node-1: `allocation_tier=premium`, 500GB disk\n- Node-2: `allocation_tier=standard`, 500GB disk\n\n**Index Setup**:\n- `critical-index`: priority=100\n- `cache-index`: priority=10\n\n**Allocation Behavior**:\n1. `critical-index` shards allocate first and prefer Node-1 (premium)\n2. `cache-index` shards allocate to remaining space, can use Node-2 (standard)\n3. If Node-1 fills up, `critical-index` shards still allocate to Node-2\n\n### Example 2: Awareness + Priority\n\n**Cluster Setup**:\n- Rack A: premium-node-1, standard-node-2\n- Rack B: premium-node-3, standard-node-4\n\n**Replication Constraint**: Replicas must be on different racks\n**High-Priority Shard**: priority=90\n\n**Allocation**:\n1. Primary → Rack A's premium-node-1\n2. Replica → Rack B's premium-node-3 (respects awareness + preference)\n\n### Example 3: Degradation Under Load\n\n**Cluster Setup**:\n- 2 premium nodes (90% full)\n- 3 standard nodes (10% full)\n\n**Incoming Shard**: priority=100\n\n**Behavior**:\n1. Try premium nodes first (via THROTTLE decision)\n2. Premium nodes full, allocation blocked\n3. Fall back to standard nodes (via force-allocate or next phase)\n4. Shard allocates to standard node (graceful degradation)\n\n---\n\n## 8. Backward Compatibility\n\n### 8.1 Default Behavior\n\n- Feature disabled by default (opt-in via settings)\n- All indices default to priority=50 (middle of range)\n- Nodes without `allocation_tier` attribute treated as standard tier\n- Existing allocation logic unchanged if feature is disabled\n\n### 8.2 Upgrade Path\n\n1. Deploy new Elasticsearch version\n2. Feature defaults to enabled but with neutral priority thresholds\n3. No change to existing cluster behavior\n4. Operators gradually enable priority constraints\n\n---\n\n## 9. Observability & Debugging\n\n### 9.1 Logging\n\nPriorityAllocationDecider logs at TRACE level:\n```\n[priority_allocation] shard [index/1], priority=90, trying node [node-1], tier=premium -> allowed\n[priority_allocation] shard [index/1], priority=90, trying node [node-2], tier=standard -> throttled (prefer premium)\n```\n\n### 9.2 Explain API\n\nExample query:\n```bash\nGET /_cluster/allocation/explain\n{\n  \"index\": \"critical-app\",\n  \"shard\": 0,\n  \"primary\": true\n}\n```\n\n### 9.3 Metrics\n\n(Future enhancement):\n- `elasticsearch.routing.allocation.priority.high_tier_allocations` - count of high-priority shards on premium nodes\n- `elasticsearch.routing.allocation.priority.degraded_allocations` - count of high-priority shards on standard nodes\n\n---\n\n## 10. Security Considerations\n\n1. **Index Priority as User Input**: Index priority is settable by any user with index permissions\n   - **Mitigation**: Operators can cap priority via role-based settings restrictions\n\n2. **Resource Starvation**: Low-priority indices might starve for premium node resources\n   - **Mitigation**: Set reasonable defaults; operators can adjust thresholds\n\n3. **Audit Trail**: Track priority-based allocation decisions for compliance\n   - **Mitigation**: Enhanced logging + explain API output\n\n---\n\n## 11. Performance Considerations\n\n1. **Shard Queue Sorting** (in PriorityShardsAllocator):\n   - O(n log n) operation once per allocation round\n   - n = number of unassigned shards (typically < 1000 in most clusters)\n   - Negligible impact\n\n2. **Decider Invocation** (in PriorityAllocationDecider):\n   - O(1) per shard-node pair (simple priority lookup + tier check)\n   - No additional cluster state lookups\n\n3. **Overall Impact**: Negligible; allocation is already O(n²) in worst case\n\n---\n\n## 12. Testing Strategy\n\n### Unit Tests\n- PriorityAllocationDecider decision logic\n- Priority extraction from IndexMetadata\n- Node tier classification\n\n### Integration Tests\n- Multi-node cluster allocation scenarios\n- Mixed priority indices\n- Awareness + priority interactions\n- Disk threshold + priority interactions\n\n### Chaos Tests\n- Node failures with high/low-priority indices\n- Dynamic priority/tier changes\n- Large unassigned shard queues\n\n---\n\n## 13. References\n\n### Existing Components\n- **ShardsAllocator**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/allocator/ShardsAllocator.java`\n- **BalancedShardsAllocator**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/allocator/BalancedShardsAllocator.java`\n- **AllocationDecider**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/AllocationDecider.java`\n- **AllocationDeciders**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/AllocationDeciders.java`\n- **DiskThresholdDecider**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/DiskThresholdDecider.java`\n- **AwarenessAllocationDecider**: `server/src/main/java/.../allocation/decider/AwarenessAllocationDecider.java`\n\n### Configuration\n- **IndexMetadata**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/IndexMetadata.java` (settings definitions)\n- **ClusterSettings**: `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/common/settings/ClusterSettings.java` (cluster-wide settings)\n\n### Testing\n- **DesiredBalanceComputerTests**: `server/src/test/java/.../allocation/allocator/DesiredBalanceComputerTests.java`\n- **AwarenessAllocationDeciderTests**: (search tests directory)\n\n---\n\n## 14. Shards with higher priority\n * are processed first, ensuring critical indices receive \"first pick\" of available slots.\n * </p>\n *\n * <p>\n * This allocator works in conjunction with {@link PriorityAllocationDecider} to:\n * <ul>\n *   <li>Reorder the shard assignment queue by priority (highest-priority shards first)</li>\n *   <li>Delegate balancing decisions to the wrapped BalancedShardsAllocator</li>\n *   <li>Support soft constraints via THROTTLE decisions from PriorityAllocationDecider</li>\n * </ul>\n * </p>\n *\n * <h2>Configuration</h2>\n *\n * Enable or disable priority-aware allocation:\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled: true  (default)\n * </pre>\n *\n * Control which node tiers are considered premium:\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers: [premium, hot]\n * </pre>\n *\n * Set the priority threshold above which to prefer premium nodes:\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold: 70\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h2>Index Settings</h2>\n *\n * Set index priority for shard allocation preferences:\n * <pre>\n * PUT /myindex/_settings\n * {\n *   \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 100\n * }\n * </pre>\n *\n * @see PriorityAllocationDecider\n * @see BalancedShardsAllocator\n * @see IndexMetadata#INDEX_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_SETTING\n */\npublic class PriorityShardsAllocator implements ShardsAllocator {\n\n    private static final Logger logger = LogManager.getLogger(PriorityShardsAllocator.class);\n\n    /**\n     * Cluster-level setting to enable/disable priority-aware allocation.\n     * Defaults to true; set to false to revert to standard balanced allocation.\n     */\n    public static final Setting<Boolean> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_ENABLED = Setting.boolSetting(\n        \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled\",\n        true,\n        Property.Dynamic,\n        Property.NodeScope\n    );\n\n    /**\n     * Cluster-level setting specifying which node tier names are considered premium.\n     * Shards with high priority prefer allocation to premium tier nodes.\n     * Example: [\"premium\", \"hot\"]\n     */\n    public static final Setting<List<String>> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_PREMIUM_TIERS = Setting.stringListSetting(\n        \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers\",\n        java.util.List.of(\"premium\"),\n        Property.Dynamic,\n        Property.NodeScope\n    );\n\n    /**\n     * Cluster-level setting specifying the priority threshold above which\n     * to apply premium tier preferences. configuration\n        this.priorityAllocationEnabled = CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_ENABLED.get(clusterSettings.getSettings());\n        this.premiumTiers = CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_PREMIUM_TIERS.get(clusterSettings.getSettings());\n        this.priorityThreshold = CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_THRESHOLD.get(clusterSettings.getSettings());\n\n        // Register listeners for dynamic updates\n        clusterSettings.addSettingsUpdateConsumer(\n // No shards to sort\n            }\n\n            // Create a mutable list of shards sorted by priority (descending)\n            List<ShardRouting> shardsToSort = new ArrayList<>(unassignedShards.size());\n            for (ShardRouting shard : unassignedShards) {\n                shardsToSort.add(shard);\n            }\n\n            // Sort by index priority (descending: highest priority first)\n            shardsToSort.sort(\n                Comparator.comparingInt(\n                    (ShardRouting shard) -> getIndexPriority(allocation, shard)\n                ).reversed()\n            );\n\n            // Replace the unassigned shards iterator with sorted shards\n            // This requires calling internal routing nodes methods\n            // Note: This is pseudocode; continuing with standard order\", e);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Reorders the unassigned shards in the routing nodes to match the priority-sorted list.\n     * This is a placeholder for the actual reordering implementation, which depends on\n     * the RoutingNodes API.\n     *\n     * @param allocation the routing allocation state\n     * @param sortedShards the shards sorted by priority (descending)\n     */\n    private void reorderUnassignedShards(RoutingAllocation allocation, List<ShardRouting> sortedShards) {\n        // TODO: Implement actual reordering using RoutingNodes API\n        // This may involve:\n        // 1. allocation.nodes().get(nodeName);\n            // if (node == null) return false;\n            // String nodeTier = node.getAttributes().get(\"allocation_tier\");\n            // return premiumTiers.contains(nodeTier);\n            return false; // placeholder\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            logger.warn(\"Failed to determine tier for node [{}], assuming standard\", nodeName, e);\n            return false; // assume standard on error\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ============= Configuration Update Methods =============\n\n    /**\n     * Called when the priority org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.allocation.decider;\n\nimport org.apache.logging.log4j.LogManager;\nimport org.apache.logging.log4j.Logger;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.metadata.IndexMetadata;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.RoutingNode;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.ShardRouting;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.allocation.RoutingAllocation;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.ClusterSettings;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.Setting;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.Setting.Property;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.Settings;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\n/**\n * {@link AllocationDecider} that implements priority-based shard allocation constraints.\n *\n * <p>\n * This decider allows Elasticsearch to preferentially allocate shards from high-priority\n * indices to high-performance (premium-tier) nodes, while gracefully degrading to\n * standard-tier nodes when necessary.\n * </p>\n *\n * <h2>Decision Logic</h2>\n *\n * For each shard allocation attempt:\n * <ol>\n *   <li>If priority allocation is disabled, return {@link Decision#ALWAYS} (no constraints)</li>\n *   <li>Retrieve the index's priority level from its metadata</li>\n *   <li>Classify the target node as premium or standard tier</li>\n *   <li>Apply decision rules based on priority and tier:\n *     <ul>\n *       <li>High priority (>= threshold) on premium tier → {@link Decision#YES}</li>\n *       <li>High priority (>= threshold) on standard tier → {@link Decision#THROTTLE}\n *           (soft constraint; allow but prefer premium)</li>\n *       <li>Low priority (< threshold) on any tier → {@link Decision#YES}</li>\n *     </ul>\n *   </li>\n * </ol>\n * </p>\n *\n * <h2>Configuration</h2>\n *\n * Enable/disable the decider (cluster-level):\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled: true\n * </pre>\n *\n * Define premium tier node attributes:\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers: [premium, hot]\n * </pre>\n *\n * Set priority threshold for soft constraints:\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold: 70\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h2>Index Configuration</h2>\n *\n * Set index priority (0-100, default 50):\n * <pre>\n * PUT /myindex/_settings\n * {\n *   \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 100\n * }\n * </pre>\n *\n * Node tier classification (set in elasticsearch.yml or via node API):\n * <pre>\n * node.attr.allocation_tier: premium  # or \"standard\", \"basic\", etc.\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h2>Interaction with Other Deciders</h2>\n *\n * PriorityAllocationDecider is typically placed in the decider chain after:\n * <ul>\n *   <li>EnableAllocationDecider (check if allocation is enabled)</li>\n *   <li>DiskThresholdDecider (check if node has disk space)</li>\n *   <li>AwarenessAllocationDecider (check rack/zone constraints)</li>\n *   <li>FilterAllocationDecider (check include/exclude/require filters)</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * This ensures that hard constraints (disk space, awareness) take precedence over\n * priority preferences.\n *\n * <h2>Examples</h2>\n *\n * <h3>Example 1: High-Priority Index Prefers Premium Nodes</h3>\n * <pre>\n * Cluster:\n *   - node-1: tier=premium (100MB free)\n *   - node-2: tier=standard (500MB free)\n *\n * Index: critical-app\n *   - priority=90\n *   - decision for node-1: YES (high priority + premium tier)\n *   - decision for node-2: THROTTLE (high priority + standard tier; suboptimal)\n *\n * Result: Shard allocates to node-1 (preferred)\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h3>Example 2: Low-Priority Index No Preference</h3>\n * <pre>\n * Index: cache-index\n *   - priority=10\n *   - decision for any node: YES (below threshold)\n *\n * Result: Shard allocates based on balanced allocation (no priority constraint)\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h3>Example 3: Degradation When Premium Nodes Full</h3>\n * <pre>\n * Cluster:\n *   - node-1: tier=premium (FULL - disk threshold breach)\n *   - node-2: tier=standard (500MB free)\n *\n * Index: critical-app\n *   - priority=90\n *   - decision for node-1: NO (DiskThresholdDecider blocks)\n *   - decision for node-2: THROTTLE (high priority but suboptimal tier)\n *\n * Result: Shard allocates to node-2 (graceful degradation)\n * </pre>\n *\n * @see PriorityShardsAllocator\n * @see AllocationDecider\n * @see Decision\n */\npublic class PriorityAllocationDecider extends AllocationDecider {\n\n    private static final Logger logger = LogManager.getLogger(PriorityAllocationDecider.class);\n\n    public static final String NAME = \"priority_allocation\";\n\n    /**\n     * Cluster-level setting to enable/disable priority-aware allocation constraints.\n     * When disabled, this decider returns {@link Decision#ALWAYS} for all decisions.\n     * Default: true\n     */\n    public static final Setting<Boolean> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_ENABLED = Setting.boolSetting(\n        \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled\",\n        true,\n        Property.Dynamic,\n        Property.NodeScope\n    );\n\n    /**\n     * Cluster-level setting listing the node tier names considered \"premium\".\n     * Nodes with the `allocation_tier` <pre>\n     * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers: [premium, hot]\n     * </pre>\n     */\n    public static final Setting<List<String>> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_PREMIUM_TIERS = Setting.stringListSetting(\n        \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers\",\n        java.util.List.of(\"premium\"),\n        Property.Dynamic,\n        Property.NodeScope\n    );\n\n    /**\n     * Cluster-level setting specifying the priority threshold above which\n     * the decider applies soft constraints (THROTTLE) for standard-tier nodes.\n     * Shards from indices with priority >= this threshold prefer premium nodes,\n     * but can still allocate to standard nodes (via THROTTLE decision).\n     *\n     * Range: 0-100\n     * Default: 70\n     *\n     * Example:\n     * <pre>\n     * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold: 70\n     * </pre>\n     */\n    public static final Setting<Integer> CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_THRESHOLD = Setting.intSetting(\n        \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold\",\n        70,\n        0,\n        100,\n        Property.Dynamic,\n        Property.NodeScope\n    );\n\n    // Volatile fields for dynamic configuration updates\n    private volatile boolean priorityAllocationEnabled;\n    private volatile List<String> premiumTiers;\n    private volatile int priorityThreshold;\n\n    /**\n     * Creates a new PriorityAllocationDecider with default settings.\n     */\n    public PriorityAllocationDecider() {\n        this.priorityAllocationEnabled = true;\n        this.premiumTiers = java.util.List.of(\"premium\");\n        this.priorityThreshold = 70;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Creates a new PriorityAllocationDecider with settings from the provided\n     * configuration. Registers dynamic setting listeners for configuration updates.\n     *\n     * @param settings the node settings (unused; settings come from clusterSettings)\n     * @param clusterSettings the cluster settings to watch for updates\n     */\n    public PriorityAllocationDecider(Settings settings, ClusterSettings clusterSettings) {\n        // Initialize from cluster settings\n        this.priorityAllocationEnabled = CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_ENABLED.get(settings);\n        this.premiumTiers = CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_PREMIUM_TIERS.get(settings);\n        this.priorityThreshold = CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_THRESHOLD.get(settings);\n\n        // Register listeners for dynamic updates\n        clusterSettings.addSettingsUpdateConsumer(\n            CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_ENABLED,\n            this::setPriorityAllocationEnabled\n        );\n        clusterSettings.addSettingsUpdateConsumer(\n            CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_PREMIUM_TIERS,\n            this::setPremiumTiers\n        );\n        clusterSettings.addSettingsUpdateConsumer(\n            CLUSTER_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY_THRESHOLD,\n            this::setPriorityThreshold\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Decides whether a shard can be allocated to a specific node.\n     *\n     * <p>\n     * Decision logic:\n     * <ul>\n     *   <li>If priority allocation is disabled, return YES (always allow)</li>\n     *   <li>Get index priority from metadata (default 50 if not set)</li>\n     *   <li>Determine if node is premium tier</li>\n     *   <li>Apply priority-based constraints:\n     *     <ul>\n     *       <li>If priority >= threshold AND node is premium: YES</li>\n     *       <li>If priority >= threshold AND node is standard: THROTTLE (soft constraint)</li>\n     *       <li>If priority < threshold: YES (no constraint)</li>\n     *     </ul>\n     *   </li>\n     * </ul>\n     * </p>\n     *\n     * @param shardRouting the shard being allocated\n     * @param node the target node for allocation\n     * @param allocation the current routing allocation state\n     * @return the allocation decision (YES, THROTTLE, or NO)\n     */\n    @Override\n    public Decision canAllocate(ShardRouting shardRouting, RoutingNode node, RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        if (!priorityAllocationEnabled) {\n            return Decision.ALWAYS; // Feature disabled; no constraints\n        }\n\n        // Extract index priority\n        IndexMetadata indexMetadata = allocation.metadata().getIndexSafe(shardRouting.index());\n        int indexPriority = getIndexPriority(indexMetadata);\n\n        // Check if index priority warrants tier-based constraints\n        if (indexPriority < priorityThreshold) {\n            // Low priority; no constraint\n            if (logger.isTraceEnabled()) {\n                logger.trace(\n                    \"Shard [{}] has priority {} (below threshold {}); no priority-based constraint\",\n                    shardRouting.shardId(),\n                    indexPriority,\n                    priorityThreshold\n                );\n            }\n            return Decision.ALWAYS;\n        }\n\n        // High priority; check node tier\n        boolean isNodePremium = isNodePremiumTier(node, allocation);\n        if (isNodePremium) {\n            // Preferred allocation\n            if (logger.isTraceEnabled()) {\n                logger.trace(\n                    \"Shard [{}] has high priority {}; allocating to premium node [{}]\",\n                    shardRouting.shardId(),\n                    indexPriority,\n                    node.nodeId()\n                );\n            }\n            return allocation.decision(\n                Decision.YES,\n                NAME,\n                \"shard [%s] has high priority [%d]; premium tier node [%s]\",\n                shardRouting.shardId(),\n                indexPriority,\n                node.nodeId()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // Suboptimal allocation; allow with THROTTLE (soft constraint)\n            if (logger.isTraceEnabled()) {\n                logger.trace(\n                    \"Shard [{}] has high priority {} but standard node [{}]; throttling\",\n                    shardRouting.shardId(),\n                    indexPriority,\n                    node.nodeId()\n                );\n            }\n            return allocation.decision(\n                Decision.THROTTLE,\n                NAME,\n                \"shard [%s] has high priority [%d]; standard tier node [%s] (prefer premium)\",\n                shardRouting.shardId(),\n                indexPriority,\n                node.nodeId()\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Decides whether a shard can remain on its current node.\n     * Uses the same logic as canAllocate() since priority constraints apply equally.\n     *\n     * @param indexMetadata the index metadata\n     * @param shardRouting the shard currently residing on the node\n     * @param node the node where the shard currently resides\n     * @param allocation the current routing allocation state\n     * @return the allocation decision\n     */\n    @Override\n    public Decision canRemain(\n        IndexMetadata indexMetadata,\n        ShardRouting shardRouting,\n        RoutingNode node,\n        RoutingAllocation allocation\n    ) {\n        // For residency decisions, use the same priority constraints as allocation\n        // A high-priority shard may be encouraged to move to a premium node if\n        // it's currently on a standard node (handled via rebalancing, not here)\n        return canAllocate(shardRouting, node, allocation);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Extracts the priority value for an index from its metadata.\n     * Falls back to default priority if not explicitly set.\n     *\n     * @param indexMetadata the index metadata\n     * @return the index priority (0-100), or default 50 if not set\n     */\n    private int getIndexPriority(IndexMetadata indexMetadata) {\n        try {\n            // TODO: Extract priority from index settings\n            // Pseudo-code:\n            // String priorityKey = PriorityShardsAllocator.INDEX_ROUTING_ALLOCATION_PRIORITY;\n            // return indexMetadata.getSettings().getAsInt(priorityKey, 50);\n            return 50; // placeholder\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            logger.warn(\"Failed to retrieve priority for index [{}], using default\", indexMetadata.getIndex().getName(), e);\n            return 50; // default on error\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Determines if a node is classified as a premium-tier node.\n     * Checks the node's attributes for a tier classification and verifies\n     * if it matches one of the configured premium tiers.\n     *\n     * @param node the routing node to check\n     * @param allocation the routing allocation state (contains node metadata)\n     * @return true if the node is classified as premium tier, false otherwise\n     */\n    private boolean isNodePremiumTier(RoutingNode node, RoutingAllocation allocation) {\n        try {\n            // TODO: Extract node tier attribute and check against premiumTiers\n            // Pseudo-code:\n            // var discoveryNode = allocation.nodes().get(node.nodeId());\n            // if (discoveryNode == null) return false;\n            // var nodeAttributes = discoveryNode.getAttributes();\n            // String nodeTier = nodeAttributes.getOrDefault(\"allocation_tier\", \"standard\");\n            // return premiumTiers.contains(nodeTier);\n            return false; // placeholder\n        } catch (Exception e) {\n            logger.warn(\"Failed to determine tier for node [{}], assuming standard\", node.nodeId(), e);\n            return false; // assume standard on error\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ============= Configuration Update Methods =============\n\n    /**\n     * Called when the org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.allocation.decider;\n\nimport org.apache.logging.log4j.LogManager;\nimport org.apache.logging.log4j.Logger;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.metadata.IndexMetadata;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.RoutingNode;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.ShardRouting;\nimport org.elasticsearch.cluster.routing.allocation.RoutingAllocation;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.ClusterSettings;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.Setting;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.Setting.Property;\nimport org.elasticsearch.common.settings.Settings;\n\nimport java.util.List;\n\n/**\n * {@link AllocationDecider} that implements priority-based shard allocation constraints.\n *\n * <p>\n * This decider allows Elasticsearch to preferentially allocate shards from high-priority\n * indices to high-performance (premium-tier) nodes, while gracefully degrading to\n * standard-tier nodes when necessary.\n * </p>\n *\n * <h2>Decision Logic</h2>\n *\n * For each shard allocation attempt:\n * <ol>\n *   <li>If priority allocation is disabled, return {@link Decision#ALWAYS} (no constraints)</li>\n *   <li>Retrieve the index's priority level from its metadata</li>\n *   <li>Classify the target node as premium or standard tier</li>\n *   <li>Apply decision rules based on priority and tier:\n *     <ul>\n *       <li>High priority (>= threshold) on premium tier → {@link Decision#YES}</li>\n *       <li>High priority (>= threshold) on standard tier → {@link Decision#THROTTLE}\n *           (soft constraint; allow but prefer premium)</li>\n *       <li>Low priority (< threshold) on any tier → {@link Decision#YES}</li>\n *     </ul>\n *   </li>\n * </ol>\n * </p>\n *\n * <h2>Configuration</h2>\n *\n * Enable/disable the decider (cluster-level):\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled: true\n * </pre>\n *\n * Define premium tier node attributes:\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers: [premium, hot]\n * </pre>\n *\n * Set priority threshold for soft constraints:\n * <pre>\n * cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold: 70\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h2>Index Configuration</h2>\n *\n * Set index priority (0-100, default 50):\n * <pre>\n * PUT /myindex/_settings\n * {\n *   \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 100\n * }\n * </pre>\n *\n * Node tier classification (set in elasticsearch.yml or via node API):\n * <pre>\n * node.attr.allocation_tier: premium  # or \"standard\", \"basic\", etc.\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h2>Interaction with Other Deciders</h2>\n *\n * PriorityAllocationDecider is typically placed in the decider chain after:\n * <ul>\n *   <li>EnableAllocationDecider (check if allocation is enabled)</li>\n *   <li>DiskThresholdDecider (check if node has disk space)</li>\n *   <li>AwarenessAllocationDecider (check rack/zone constraints)</li>\n *   <li>FilterAllocationDecider (check include/exclude/require filters)</li>\n * </ul>\n *\n * This ensures that hard constraints (disk space, awareness) take precedence over\n * priority preferences.\n *\n * <h2>Examples</h2>\n *\n * <h3>Example 1: High-Priority Index Prefers Premium Nodes</h3>\n * <pre>\n * Cluster:\n *   - node-1: tier=premium (100MB free)\n *   - node-2: tier=standard (500MB free)\n *\n * Index: critical-app\n *   - priority=90\n *   - decision for node-1: YES (high priority + premium tier)\n *   - decision for node-2: THROTTLE (high priority + standard tier; suboptimal)\n *\n * Result: Shard allocates to node-1 (preferred)\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h3>Example 2: Low-Priority Index No Preference</h3>\n * <pre>\n * Index: cache-index\n *   - priority=10\n *   - decision for any node: YES (below threshold)\n *\n * Result: Shard allocates based on balanced allocation (no priority constraint)\n * </pre>\n *\n * <h3>Example 3: Degradation When Premium Nodes Full</h3>\n * <pre>\n * Cluster:\n *   - node-1: tier=premium (FULL - disk threshold breach)\n *   - node-2: tier=standard (500MB free)\n *\n * Index: critical-app\n *   - priority=90\n *   - decision for node-1: NO (DiskThresholdDecider blocks)\n *   - decision for node-2: THROTTLE (high priority but suboptimal tier)\n *\n * Result: Shard allocates to node-2 (graceful degradation)\n * </pre>\n *\n * @see PriorityShardsAllocator\n * @see AllocationDecider\n * @see Decision\n */\npublic class PriorityAllocationDecider * <p>\n     * Decision logic:\n     * <ul>\n     *   <li>If priority allocation is disabled, return YES (always allow)</li>\n     *   <li>Get index priority from metadata (default 50 if not set)</li>\n     *   <li>Determine if node is premium tier</li>\n     *   <li>Apply priority-based constraints:\n     *     <ul>\n     *       <li>If priority >= threshold AND node is premium: constraints\n        if (indexPriority < priorityThreshold) {\n            // Low priority; no constraint\n            if (logger.isTraceEnabled()) {\n                logger.trace(\n indexPriority,\n                node.nodeId()\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Decides whether a shard can remain on its current node.\n     * Uses the same logic as canAllocate() constraints as allocation\n        // A high-priority shard may be encouraged to move to a premium node if\n        // it's currently on a standard node (handled via rebalancing, not here)\n        return canAllocate(shardRouting, node, allocation);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Extracts the priority value for an index from its metadata.\n     * Falls back to default priority if not explicitly set.\n     *\n     * @param indexMetadata the index metadata\n     * @return the index priority (0-100), or default 50 if not set\n     */\n    private int getIndexPriority(IndexMetadata indexMetadata) {\n        try {\n            // TODO: Extract priority from index settings\n            // Pseudo-code:\n            // String priorityKey = (Exception e) {\n            logger.warn(\"Failed to retrieve priority for index [{}], using default\", indexMetadata.getIndex().getName(), e);\n            return 50; // default on error\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Determines if a node is classified as a premium-tier node.\n     * Checks the node's attributes for a tier classification and verifies\n     * if it matches one of the configured premium tiers.\n     *\n     * @param node the routing node to check\n     * @param allocation the routing allocation state (contains node metadata)\n     * @return true if the node is {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_01EdKXszji6FnrC2BXDe8MXE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GvLBTSik3u5y4qX4H3P6T9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/README.md","content":"# Priority-Based Shard Allocation Strategy for Elasticsearch\n\n## Overview\n\nThis directory contains the design and skeleton implementation for a **priority-based shard allocation strategy** for Elasticsearch v8.17.0. The design enables critical indices to be preferentially placed on faster (premium-tier) nodes while maintaining graceful degradation when premium capacity is exhausted.\n\n## Deliverables\n\n### 1. **design_doc.md** - Comprehensive Design Document\n\nA complete design specification covering:\n\n- **Current Architecture Overview**: Detailed analysis of existing allocation framework\n  - ShardsAllocator interface and BalancedShardsAllocator implementation\n  - AllocationDecider base class and composition pattern\n  - Existing decider examples (DiskThresholdDecider, AwarenessAllocationDecider)\n\n- **Problem Statement**: Why priority-aware allocation is needed\n  - Current lack of performance-tier awareness\n  - Limitation in handling critical vs. non-critical indices\n\n- **Proposed Design**: Four-component solution\n  - `index.routing.allocation.priority` setting (0-100, default 50)\n  - `node.attr.allocation_tier` attribute (e.g., \"premium\", \"standard\")\n  - `PriorityAllocationDecider` - enforces priority constraints\n  - `PriorityShardsAllocator` - reorders shards by priority for allocation\n\n- **Integration Points**: How new components compose with existing allocation framework\n  - Placement in the decider chain\n  - Interaction with DiskThresholdDecider, AwarenessAllocationDecider, FilterAllocationDecider\n  - Backward compatibility and graceful degradation\n\n- **API Specification**: New settings and configuration\n  - Index settings: `index.routing.allocation.priority`\n  - Cluster settings: `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.*`\n  - Cluster Explain API enhancements\n\n- **Implementation Roadmap**: 4-phase development plan\n- **Behavior Examples**: Real-world scenarios with expected outcomes\n- **Testing Strategy**: Unit, integration, and chaos test plans\n- **References**: Links to relevant Elasticsearch source files\n\n**Key Design Principles**:\n1. Non-intrusive - integrates without major refactoring\n2. Composable - works alongside existing deciders\n3. Configurable - allows operator control and tuning\n4. Observable - clear logging and explain API output\n5. Safe - graceful degradation without data loss\n\n### 2. **PriorityShardsAllocator.java** - Skeleton Implementation\n\nA Java class implementing the `ShardsAllocator` interface that:\n\n- **Wraps BalancedShardsAllocator**: Delegates actual balancing decisions\n- **Sorts Shards by Priority**: Reorders unassigned shards queue (highest priority first)\n- **Supports Async Allocation**: Implements both sync and async allocation methods\n- **Dynamic Configuration**: Watches for runtime setting changes\n- **Graceful Error Handling**: Continues with standard allocation on errors\n\n**Key Methods**:\n- `allocate(RoutingAllocation)` - sorts shards then delegates\n- `allocate(RoutingAllocation, ActionListener<Void>)` - async variant\n- `sortUnassignedShardsByPriority()` - reorders shard queue\n- `getIndexPriority()` - extracts priority from index metadata\n- `isNodePremiumTier()` - classifies node as premium/standard\n\n**Settings Exposed**:\n- `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled` (Boolean, default: true)\n- `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers` (List[String], default: [\"premium\"])\n- `cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold` (Integer 0-100, default: 70)\n\n### 3. **PriorityAllocationDecider.java** - Skeleton Implementation\n\nA Java class extending `AllocationDecider` that:\n\n- **Enforces Priority Constraints**: Returns YES/THROTTLE/NO based on priority and tier\n- **Decision Logic**:\n  - High-priority shard on premium node → YES\n  - High-priority shard on standard node → THROTTLE (soft constraint)\n  - Low-priority shard on any node → YES\n- **Composes with Other Deciders**: Fails fast on NO from other deciders\n- **Dynamic Configuration**: Watches for runtime setting changes\n- **Detailed Logging**: Trace-level logging for debugging allocation decisions\n\n**Key Methods**:\n- `canAllocate(ShardRouting, RoutingNode, RoutingAllocation)` - main decision logic\n- `canRemain()` - uses same logic as canAllocate\n- `getIndexPriority()` - extracts priority from index metadata\n- `isNodePremiumTier()` - classifies node tier\n\n**Settings Exposed**:\n- Same as PriorityShardsAllocator\n\n## Architecture Diagram\n\n```\nAllocationService (orchestration)\n    ↓\nShardsAllocator (pluggable)\n    ├── BalancedShardsAllocator (existing)\n    └── PriorityShardsAllocator (NEW)\n            ↓\n        [reorder shards by priority]\n            ↓\n        BalancedShardsAllocator.allocate()\n            ↓\n        AllocationDeciders chain\n            ├── EnableAllocationDecider\n            ├── DiskThresholdDecider\n            ├── AwarenessAllocationDecider\n            ├── FilterAllocationDecider\n            ├── PriorityAllocationDecider (NEW)\n            └── ... other deciders ...\n```\n\n## Design Highlights\n\n### 1. Non-Intrusive Integration\n\nThe design extends existing components rather than modifying them:\n- PriorityShardsAllocator wraps BalancedShardsAllocator (decorator pattern)\n- PriorityAllocationDecider extends AllocationDecider (template method pattern)\n- New settings follow Elasticsearch conventions\n\n### 2. Composability with Existing Deciders\n\nPriority constraints interact safely with other constraints:\n- **DiskThreshold** (hard constraint): Disk space checks take precedence\n- **Awareness** (hard constraint): Rack/zone distribution respected within priority preferences\n- **Filter** (hard constraint): Include/exclude/require filters applied first\n- **Priority** (soft constraint): THROTTLE signals preference, not requirement\n\n### 3. Graceful Degradation\n\nHigh-priority shards still allocate when premium nodes unavailable:\n- THROTTLE decision allows allocation to standard nodes\n- Falls back to standard balanced allocation\n- No starvation; all shards eventually allocate\n\n### 4. Dynamic Configuration\n\nAll settings are dynamic (cluster-scoped):\n- No cluster restart required to enable/disable\n- Can adjust premium tiers and thresholds at runtime\n- Easy A/B testing of configurations\n\n## Configuration Examples\n\n### Enable Priority Allocation\n\n```bash\nPUT /_cluster/settings\n{\n  \"persistent\": {\n    \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.enabled\": true,\n    \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.premium_tiers\": [\"premium\", \"hot\"],\n    \"cluster.routing.allocation.priority.threshold\": 70\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### Configure Node Tiers\n\nIn `elasticsearch.yml`:\n```yaml\n# Premium nodes (fast SSD, 256GB RAM)\nnode.name: premium-node-1\nnode.attr.allocation_tier: premium\n\n# Standard nodes (regular SSD, 64GB RAM)\nnode.name: standard-node-1\nnode.attr.allocation_tier: standard\n```\n\n### Set Index Priority\n\n```bash\nPUT /critical-app/_settings\n{\n  \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 100\n}\n\nPUT /cache-index/_settings\n{\n  \"index.routing.allocation.priority\": 10\n}\n```\n\n## Usage Scenarios\n\n### Scenario 1: Critical Application Index\n\n```\nIndex: critical-app\n- priority: 100 (highest)\n- 3 shards, 2 replicas each\n\nCluster:\n- 2 premium nodes (fast SSD, 256GB RAM)\n- 3 standard nodes (regular SSD, 64GB RAM)\n\nResult:\n- Primary shards prefer premium nodes\n- Replica shards distributed across both tiers\n- If premium nodes full, gracefully degrade to standard\n```\n\n### Scenario 2: Multi-Tier Allocation\n\n```\nIndices:\n- prod-primary (priority: 100)  → premium nodes\n- prod-replica (priority: 80)   → premium or standard\n- staging (priority: 40)        → any node\n- logs (priority: 10)           → standard nodes\n\nAllocation Strategy:\n1. Assign prod-primary shards first (highest priority)\n2. Fill premium nodes with prod-primary shards\n3. Assign prod-replica to remaining premium space + standard\n4. Assign staging to available space\n5. Pack logs into remaining capacity\n```\n\n## References to Elasticsearch Source\n\nThe skeleton code references these key Elasticsearch components:\n\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/ShardsAllocator.java`\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/allocator/BalancedShardsAllocator.java`\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AllocationDecider.java`\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AllocationDeciders.java`\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/DiskThresholdDecider.java`\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/routing/allocation/decider/AwarenessAllocationDecider.java`\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/cluster/metadata/IndexMetadata.java`\n- `server/src/main/java/org/elasticsearch/common/settings/ClusterSettings.java`\n\n## Next Steps for Implementation\n\n### Phase 1: Foundation\n1. ✅ Design document completed\n2. ✅ Skeleton code structure created\n3. ⬜ Implement priority extraction from IndexMetadata\n4. ⬜ Implement node tier classification\n5. ⬜ Register PriorityAllocationDecider in AllocationService\n\n### Phase 2: Core Functionality\n6. ⬜ Implement shard queue reordering in PriorityShardsAllocator\n7. ⬜ Implement decision logic in PriorityAllocationDecider\n8. ⬜ Register allocator in cluster initialization\n9. ⬜ Add configuration settings to ClusterSettings\n\n### Phase 3: Integration & Testing\n10. ⬜ Unit tests for both classes\n11. ⬜ Integration tests with BalancedShardsAllocator\n12. ⬜ Explain API integration\n13. ⬜ Chaos/stress testing\n\n### Phase 4: Polish & Documentation\n14. ⬜ Elasticsearch documentation\n15. ⬜ Configuration guides\n16. ⬜ Troubleshooting guides\n17. ⬜ Migration guides from other approaches\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\n/workspace/\n├── design_doc.md                    #
